# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-10)

## Corpus Check
- 5232 files · ~12,096,359 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 67637 nodes · 177822 edges · 4497 communities (1591 shown, 2906 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5454 edges (avg confidence: 0.73)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `45c5ec3e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
